# Docker has specific installation instructions for each operating system.`
# Please refer to the official documentation at https://docker.com/get-started/`
`
# Pull the Node.js Docker image:`
docker pull node:22-alpine`
`
# Create a Node.js container and start a Shell session:`
docker run -it --rm --entrypoint sh node:22-alpine`
`
# Verify the Node.js version:`
node -v # Should print "v22.20.0".`
`
# Verify npm version:`
npm -v # Should print "10.9.3".`

$env:PATH += ";C:\Program Files\nodejs"; @'`
import express from "express";`
import { getPool, sql } from "../config/db.js";`
`
const router = express.Router();`
`
const subjectsStore = [`
  {`
    id: 1,`
    subject_code: "CS301",`
    subject_name: "Data Structures",`
    sub_short_name: "DS",`
    course_code: "43543",`
    scheme_name: "N",`
    semester_number: "3",`
    year: "2",`
    subject_type: "Theory",`
    is_elective: "N",`
    credit_system: "4",`
    max_marks_internal: "50",`
    max_marks_external: "100",`
    ins_code: "INS001"`
  }`
];`
`
async function tableExists() {`
  try {`
    const pool = getPool();`
    const result = await pool.request().query(``
      SELECT COUNT(*) as count `
      FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES `
      WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'subject_master'`
    `);`
    return result.recordset[0].count > 0;`
  } catch (error) {`
    console.error("Error checking table existence:", error);`
    return false;`
  }`
}`
`
router.get("/", async (req, res) => {`
  try {`
    const { ins_code } = req.query;`
    const hasTable = await tableExists();`
    `
    if (!hasTable) {`
      let filteredSubjects = subjectsStore;`
      if (ins_code) {`
        filteredSubjects = subjectsStore.filter(s => s.ins_code === ins_code);`
      }`
      return res.json(filteredSubjects);`
    }`
`
    const pool = getPool();`
    let query = "SELECT * FROM subject_master";`
    let request = pool.request();`
    `
    if (ins_code) {`
      query += " WHERE ins_code = @ins_code";`
      request.input("ins_code", sql.NVarChar, ins_code);`
    }`
    `
    const result = await request.query(query);`
    res.json(result.recordset);`
  } catch (error) {`
    console.error("Error fetching subjects:", error);`
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch subjects" });`
  }`
});`
`
export default router;`

import fs from 'fs';`
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';`
import path from 'path';`
`
const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));`
const sqlScript = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'scripts/create-theme-table.sql'), 'utf8');`
`
async function createThemeTable() {`
  try {`
    const pool = getPool();`
    await pool.request().query(sqlScript);`
    console.log('Theme table setup completed successfully');`
  } catch (error) {`
    console.error('Error setting up theme table:', error);`
  }`
}`

import { getPool } from './config/db.js';`
import fs from 'fs';`
`
async function insertSampleAdmin() {`
  try {`
    const pool = getPool();`
    const sqlScript = fs.readFileSync('./scripts/insert-sample-admin.sql', 'utf8');`
    const queries = sqlScript.split(';').filter(q => q.trim().length > 0);`
    `
    for (const query of queries) {`
      if (query.trim()) {`
        console.log('Executing:', query.trim().substring(0, 50) + '...');`
        const result = await pool.request().query(query.trim());`
        if (result.recordset && result.recordset.length > 0) {`
          console.log('Result:', result.recordset);`
        }`
      }`
    }`
    console.log(' Sample admin data inserted successfully');`
  } catch (error) {`
    console.error(' Error:', error.message);`
  }`
}`
`
insertSampleAdmin();`

import { getPool } from './config/db.js';`
`
async function insertSampleAdmin() {`
  try {`
    const pool = getPool();`
    `
    // First check if data exists`
    const checkResult = await pool.request()`
      .input('ins_code', 'INS001')`
      .query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM admin_master WHERE ins_code = @ins_code');`
    `
    console.log('Current admin records for INS001:', checkResult.recordset[0].count);`
    `
    if (checkResult.recordset[0].count === 0) {`
      // Insert sample data`
      const insertResult = await pool.request()`
        .input('profile_id', 'PROF001')`
        .input('ins_code', 'INS001')`
        .input('full_name', 'rajkumar')`
        .input('designation', 'principal')`
        .input('email', 'christypunitha.jriit@gmail.com')`
        .input('phone_number', '+91 8807054164')`
        .input('employee_id', '0001')`
        .query(``
          INSERT INTO admin_master (profile_id, ins_code, full_name, designation, email, phone_number, employee_id)`
          VALUES (@profile_id, @ins_code, @full_name, @designation, @email, @phone_number, @employee_id)`
        `);`
      `
      console.log(' Sample admin data inserted');`
    }`
    `
    // Verify the insert`
    const result = await pool.request()`
      .input('ins_code', 'INS001')`
      .query('SELECT * FROM admin_master WHERE ins_code = @ins_code');`
    `
    console.log('Admin data:', result.recordset);`
    `
  } catch (error) {`
    console.error(' Error:', error.message);`
  }`
}`
`
insertSampleAdmin();`

node -e "`
import { getPool } from './config/db.js';`
`
async function insertSampleAdmin() {`
  try {`
    const pool = getPool();`
    `
    // First check if data exists`
    const checkResult = await pool.request()`
      .input('ins_code', 'INS001')`
      .query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM admin_master WHERE ins_code = @ins_code');`
    `
    console.log('Current admin records for INS001:', checkResult.recordset[0].count);`
    `
    if (checkResult.recordset[0].count === 0) {`
      // Insert sample data`
      const insertQuery = `INSERT INTO admin_master (profile_id, ins_code, full_name, designation, email, phone_number, employee_id) VALUES (@profile_id, @ins_code, @full_name, @designation, @email, @phone_number, @employee_id)`;`
      `
      const insertResult = await pool.request()`
        .input('profile_id', 'PROF001')`
        .input('ins_code', 'INS001')`
        .input('full_name', 'rajkumar')`
        .input('designation', 'principal')`
        .input('email', 'christypunitha.jriit@gmail.com')`
        .input('phone_number', '+91 8807054164')`
        .input('employee_id', '0001')`
        .query(insertQuery);`
      `
      console.log(' Sample admin data inserted');`
    }`
    `
    // Verify the insert`
    const result = await pool.request()`
      .input('ins_code', 'INS001')`
      .query('SELECT * FROM admin_master WHERE ins_code = @ins_code');`
    `
    console.log('Admin data:', result.recordset);`
    `
  } catch (error) {`
    console.error(' Error:', error.message);`
  }`
}`
`
insertSampleAdmin();`

`
async function checkData() {`
  try {`
    await connectDB();`
    const pool = getPool();`
    `
    const students = await pool.request()`

      .query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM faculty_master WHERE ins_code = @ins_code');`
      `
    const courses = await pool.request()`
      .input('ins_code', sql.NVarChar(50), 'INS003')`
      .query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM course_master WHERE ins_code = @ins_code');`

    console.log('Current data for INS003:');`
    console.log('Students:', students.recordset[0].count);`
    console.log('Faculty:', faculty.recordset[0].count);`
    console.log('Courses:', courses.recordset[0].count);`

    console.error(' Error:', error.message);`
  }`
}`

node -e "`
const { getPool } = require('./config/db.js');`
`
async function checkAssignedExams() {`
  try {`
    const pool = getPool();`
    console.log('Checking assigned_exams table...');`
    `
    // Check table structure`
    const structureResult = await pool.request().query(``
      SELECT COLUMN_NAME, DATA_TYPE, IS_NULLABLE`
      FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS `
      WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'assigned_exams'`
      ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION`
    `);`
    `
    console.log('Table structure:');`
    structureResult.recordset.forEach(col => {`
      console.log(\`  \${col.COLUMN_NAME}: \${col.DATA_TYPE} (\${col.IS_NULLABLE})\`);`
    });`
    `
    // Check if there's any data`
    const countResult = await pool.request().query('SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM assigned_exams');`
    console.log(\`\nTotal records: \${countResult.recordset[0].total}\`);`
    `
    // Show first 5 records`
    const sampleResult = await pool.request().query(``
      SELECT TOP 5 `
        faculty_code, exam_name, student_count, batch_count, status, exam_date, course_name, subject_name`
      FROM assigned_exams `
      ORDER BY created_at DESC`
    `);`
    `
    console.log('\nSample records:');`
    sampleResult.recordset.forEach((record, index) => {`
      console.log(\`  \${index + 1}. Faculty: \${record.faculty_code}, Exam: \${record.exam_name}, Students: \${record.student_count}, Batches: \${record.batch_count}\`);`
    });`
    `
    // Check distinct faculty codes`
    const facultyResult = await pool.request().query(``
      SELECT DISTINCT faculty_code, COUNT(*) as exam_count`
      FROM assigned_exams `
      GROUP BY faculty_code`
      ORDER BY exam_count DESC`
    `);`
    `
    console.log('\nFaculty codes with exam counts:');`
    facultyResult.recordset.forEach(faculty => {`
      console.log(\`  \${faculty.faculty_code}: \${faculty.exam_count} exams\`);`
    });`
    `
  } catch (error) {`
    console.error('Error:', error.message);`
  }`
}`
`
checkAssignedExams();`

const { sql, poolPromise } = require('./config/db');`
`
async function checkTables() {`
  try {`
    const pool = await poolPromise;`
    `
    console.log(' Checking student_exam table columns:');`
    const studentExamResult = await pool.request().query(\``
      SELECT COLUMN_NAME, DATA_TYPE, IS_NULLABLE `
      FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS `
      WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'student_exam'`
      ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION`
    \`);`
    console.log('student_exam columns:', studentExamResult.recordset);`
    `
    console.log('\\n Checking exam_pause_requests table columns:');`
    const pauseRequestsResult = await pool.request().query(\``
      SELECT COLUMN_NAME, DATA_TYPE, IS_NULLABLE `
      FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS `
      WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'exam_pause_requests'`
      ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION`
    \`);`
    console.log('exam_pause_requests columns:', pauseRequestsResult.recordset);`
    `
    console.log('\\n Checking faculty_master table columns:');`
    const facultyResult = await pool.request().query(\``
      SELECT COLUMN_NAME, DATA_TYPE, IS_NULLABLE `
      FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS `
      WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'faculty_master'`
      ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION`
    \`);`
    console.log('faculty_master columns:', facultyResult.recordset);`
    `
  } catch (error) {`
    console.error('Error:', error);`
  } finally {`
    process.exit();`
  }`
}`
`
checkTables();`

import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";`
import Home from "./pages/Home.jsx";`
import Login from "./pages/Login.jsx";`
import NotFound from "./pages/NotFound.jsx";`
`
function App() {`
  return (`
    <BrowserRouter>`
      <Routes>`
        <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />`
        <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />`
        <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />`
      </Routes>`
    </BrowserRouter>`
  );`
}`
`
export default App;'

    (Get-Content $file.FullName) -replace '@/components/ui/', '@/components/' ``
                                 -replace '@/components/layout/Header', '@/components/landing/Navbar' ``
                                 -replace '@/components/layout/Footer', '@/components/landing/Footer' ``

$components = Get-ChildItem "client/src/components/*.jsx"
foreach ($comp in $components) {`
    $lowerName = $comp.BaseName.ToLower()`
    $pascalName = $comp.BaseName`
    $files = Get-ChildItem -Path "client/src" -Recurse -Include "*.jsx", "*.js"`
    foreach ($file in $files) {`
        (Get-Content $file.FullName) -replace "@/components/$lowerName`"", "@/components/$pascalName`"" | Set-Content $file.FullName`
    }`
}

node -e "const db = require('./src/config/database.js'); `
Promise.all([`
    db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM empdet WHERE CheckStatus = \'true\''),`
    db.query('SELECT SUM(NET) as total_net FROM emppay WHERE Month = MONTH(CURRENT_DATE()) AND Year = YEAR(CURRENT_DATE())'),`
    db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) as pending FROM empdet WHERE STATUS = \'Pending\''),`
    db.query('SELECT * FROM logdetails ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 5'),`
    db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) as missing_bank FROM empdet WHERE (AccountNo IS NULL OR AccountNo = \'\') AND CheckStatus = \'true\'')`
]).then((results) => {`

-- Consolidate Staff Master`
RENAME TABLE staff_master TO Staff_Master;`
ALTER TABLE Staff_Master `
  CHANGE COLUMN Mobile_No Mobile VARCHAR(255),`
  CHANGE COLUMN Email_ID Email VARCHAR(255),`
  CHANGE COLUMN Date_Of_Birth DOB DATE,`
  CHANGE COLUMN PAN_No PAN_Number VARCHAR(255),`
  CHANGE COLUMN Bank_Account_No Account_Number VARCHAR(255),`
  CHANGE COLUMN Aadhar_No Aadhar_Number VARCHAR(255);`
`
-- Ensure Library tables are consistent`
RENAME TABLE availablebooks TO AvailableBooks;`
RENAME TABLE bookissue TO BookIssue;`
RENAME TABLE borrowerdetails TO BorrowerDetails;`
RENAME TABLE currentborrowerreport TO CurrentBorrowerReport;`
RENAME TABLE currentborrowers TO CurrentBorrowers;`
RENAME TABLE duedateexitreport TO DueDateExitReport;`
RENAME TABLE finedetails TO FineDetails;`
RENAME TABLE finereport TO FineReport;`
RENAME TABLE missingbooks TO MissingBooks;`
"
cd 'c:\Users\Hp\Desktop\SF_ERP-main'
docker exec sf_erp_db mysql -uroot -pPrawin@2k4 cms -e "`
DELIMITER //`
CREATE PROCEDURE FixStaffColumns()`
BEGIN`
    -- Rename columns to match controller/staffDetails.js expectations`
    IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Mobile_No') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master CHANGE COLUMN Mobile_No Mobile VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
    IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Email_ID') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master CHANGE COLUMN Email_ID Email VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
    IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Date_Of_Birth') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master CHANGE COLUMN Date_Of_Birth DOB DATE;`
    END IF;`
    IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'PAN_No') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master CHANGE COLUMN PAN_No PAN_Number VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
    IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Bank_Account_No') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master CHANGE COLUMN Bank_Account_No Account_Number VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
    IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Aadhar_No') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master CHANGE COLUMN Aadhar_No Aadhar_Number VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
`
    -- Add missing columns used in controllers`
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Religion') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master ADD COLUMN Religion VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Community') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master ADD COLUMN Community VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Caste') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master ADD COLUMN Caste VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Temporary_Address') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master ADD COLUMN Temporary_Address TEXT;`
    END IF;`
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Permanent_Address') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master ADD COLUMN Permanent_Address TEXT;`
    END IF;`
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'cms' AND TABLE_NAME = 'Staff_Master' AND COLUMN_NAME = 'Photo') THEN`
        ALTER TABLE Staff_Master ADD COLUMN Photo VARCHAR(255);`
    END IF;`
END //`
DELIMITER ;`
CALL FixStaffColumns();`
DROP PROCEDURE FixStaffColumns;`
"

`
export const getExamDetails = async (req, res) => {`
  try {`
    const { examDate, session, subjectCode, deptCode } = req.query;`
    const tt = await ExamTimetable.findOne({ examDate, session, subCode: subjectCode, deptCode });`
    res.json(tt);`
  } catch (err) { res.status(500).json({ error: err.message }); }`
};`
`
export const getHallCapacity = async (req, res) => {`
  try {`
    const { hallCode } = req.query;`
    const hall = await HallMaster.findOne({ hallCode });`
    res.json({ capacity: hall ? hall.seatingCapacity : 0 });`
  } catch (err) { res.status(500).json({ error: err.message }); }`
};`
"@
Add-Content -Path examGenerationController.js -Value $content -Encoding utf8

`
// ── Aliases to match routes/dashboard.js ─────────────────────────────────────`
`
export const getDashboardStatsByDate = getDashboardStats;`
export const getAttendanceTrends = getAttendanceTrend;`
export const getDeptAttendanceByDate = getDeptAttendance;`
`
export const getDepartmentWiseStudents = async (req, res) => {`
    try {`
        const stats = await Student.aggregate([`
            { $match: { admissionStatus: 'Admitted' } },`
            { $group: { _id: '$deptName', count: { $sum: 1 } } },`
            { $project: { Dept_Name: '$_id', count: 1, _id: 0 } }`
        ]);`
        res.json({ success: true, data: stats });`
    } catch (err) { res.status(500).json({ error: err.message }); }`
};`
`
export const getAcademicYears = async (req, res) => {`
    try {`
        const years = await Student.distinct('academicYear');`
        res.json({ success: true, data: years.filter(y => y).map(y => ({ academicYear: y })) });`
    } catch (err) { res.status(500).json({ error: err.message }); }`
};`
`
export const getTodayAttendance = async (req, res) => {`
    return getDashboardStats(req, res);`
};`
`
export const getAttendanceDetailsByDate = async (req, res) => {`
    res.json({ success: true, present: [], absent: [] });`
};`
`
export const getAttendanceDetailsByType = async (req, res) => {`
    res.json({ success: true, onDuty: [], medicalLeave: [] });`
};`
`
export const getLibraryStats = async (req, res) => {`
    res.json({ success: true, totalBooks: 0, issuedToday: 0 });`
};`
`
export const getFeeMetrics = async (req, res) => {`
    res.json({ success: true, collectedToday: 0, pendingTotal: 0 });`
};`
`
export const getExamAnalytics = async (req, res) => {`
    res.json({ success: true, overallPassPercent: 0 });`
};`
`
export const getHealthStats = async (req, res) => {`
    res.json({ success: true, visitsToday: 0 });`
};`
`
export const getQuickStats = async (req, res) => {`
    return getDashboardStats(req, res);`
};`
